<pre>Let me analyze this protein sequence to identify which glycosyltransferase family it belongs to.

First, let me look at the overall characteristics of the protein:
- It&#x27;s a membrane protein (starts with a transmembrane domain: MMLKSCGLIFKGKRFVRLFIFIAVCLGFLIAVTILAGL)
- It has a signal anchor/transmembrane region at the N-terminus
- The rest of the sequence appears to be a catalytic domain

Now, let me consider the glycosyltransferase families:

**GT2 (Glycosyltransferase family 2):** These typically use UDP-sugars and are involved in transferring sugars. They have a characteristic ~120 amino acid region. They are relatively small domains.

**GT4 (Glycosyltransferase family 4):** These are large families, typically &gt;400 amino acids. They often have a conserved DD (aspartate-aspartate) motif that coordinates metal ions. They can be divided into subfamilies (GT4-A and GT4-B).

**GT92 (Glycosyltransferase family 92):** This is a specific family that includes proteins like human CGaLT (C-terminal GalNAc transferase) and similar proteins.
